{"parentUuid":"d3d1a001-7910-451c-bf58-700e672dd5c4","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af226031d0bcc0f86","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- heartbeat-protocol: Claude node contract for DebateAI Graph Spine v2. The Main Orchestrator (Claude-Router seat) contract; thin loader over the repo spine. All agent launches use /goal.\n- commit-commands:clean_gone: Cleans up all git branches marked as [gone] (branches that have been deleted on the remote but still exist locally), including removing associated worktrees.\n- commit-commands:commit-push-pr: Commit, push, and open a PR\n- commit-commands:commit: Create a git commit\n- pdf-viewer:annotate: Collaboratively annotate a PDF — propose markup, review together, iterate\n- pdf-viewer:fill-form: Fill PDF form fields interactively with live visual feedback\n- pdf-viewer:open: Open a PDF in the interactive viewer\n- pdf-viewer:sign: Place a signature or initials image on a PDF\n- frontend-design:frontend-design: Guidance for distinctive, intentional visual design when building new UI or reshaping an existing one. Helps with aesthetic direction, typography, and making choices that don't read as templated defaults.\n- mattpocock-skills:diagnosing-bugs: Diagnosis loop for hard bugs and performance regressions. Use when the user says \"diagnose\"/\"debug this\", or reports something broken/throwing/failing/slow.\n- mattpocock-skills:tdd: Test-driven development. Use when the user wants to build features or fix bugs test-first, mentions \"red-green-refactor\", or wants integration tests.\n- mattpocock-skills:prototype: Build a throwaway prototype to answer a design question. Use when the user wants to sanity-check whether a state model or logic feels right, or explore what a UI should look like.\n- mattpocock-skills:research: Investigate a question against high-trust primary sources and capture the findings as a Markdown file in the repo. Use when the user wants a topic researched, docs or API facts gathered, or reading legwork delegated to a background agent.\n- mattpocock-skills:domain-modeling: Build and sharpen a project's domain model. Use when the user wants to pin down domain terminology or a ubiquitous language, record an architectural decision, or when another skill needs to maintain the domain model.\n- mattpocock-skills:codebase-design: Shared vocabulary for designing deep modules. Use when the user wants to design or improve a module's interface, find deepening opportunities, decide where a seam goes, make code more testable or AI-navigable, or when another skill needs the deep-module vocabulary.\n- mattpocock-skills:code-review: Review the changes since a fixed point (commit, branch, tag, or merge-base) along two axes — Standards (does the code follow this repo's documented coding standards?) and Spec (does the code match what the originating issue/PRD asked for?). Runs both reviews in parallel sub-agents and reports them side by side. Use when the user wants to review a branch, a PR, work-in-progress changes, or asks to \"review since X\".\n- mattpocock-skills:resolving-merge-conflicts: Use when you need to resolve an in-progress git merge/rebase conflict.\n- mattpocock-skills:grilling: Grill the user relentlessly about a plan, decision, or idea. Use when the user wants to stress-test their thinking, or uses any 'grill' trigger phrases.\n- superpowers:brainstorming: You MUST use this before any creative work - creating features, building components, adding functionality, or modifying behavior. Explores user intent, requirements and design before implementation.\n- superpowers:dispatching-parallel-agents: Use when facing 2+ independent tasks that can be worked on without shared state or sequential dependencies\n- superpowers:executing-plans: Use when you have a written implementation plan to execute in a separate session with review checkpoints\n- superpowers:finishing-a-development-branch: Use when implementation is complete, all tests pass, and you need to decide how to integrate the work\n- superpowers:receiving-code-review: Use when receiving code review feedback, before implementing suggestions, especially if feedback seems unclear or technically questionable - requires technical rigor and verification, not performative agreement or blind implementation\n- superpowers:requesting-code-review: Use when completing tasks, implementing major features, or before merging to verify work meets requirements\n- superpowers:subagent-driven-development: Use when executing implementation plans with independent tasks in the current session\n- superpowers:systematic-debugging: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes\n- superpowers:test-driven-development: Use when implementing any feature or bugfix, before writing implementation code\n- superpowers:using-git-worktrees: Use when starting feature work that needs isolation from current workspace or before executing implementation plans - ensures an isolated workspace exists via native tools or git worktree fallback\n- superpowers:using-superpowers: Use when starting any conversation - establishes how to find and use skills, requiring skill invocation before ANY response including clarifying questions\n- superpowers:verification-before-completion: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always\n- superpowers:writing-plans: Use when you have a spec or requirements for a multi-step task, before touching code\n- superpowers:writing-skills: Use when creating new skills, editing existing skills, or verifying skills work before deployment\n- pdf-viewer:view-pdf: Interactive PDF viewer. Use when the user wants to open, show, or view a PDF and collaborate on it visually — annotate, highlight, stamp, fill form fields, place signature/initials, or review markup together. Not for summarization or text extraction (use native Read instead).\n- productivity:memory-management: Two-tier memory system that makes Claude a true workplace collaborator. Decodes shorthand, acronyms, nicknames, and internal language so Claude understands requests like a colleague would. CLAUDE.md for working memory, memory/ directory for the full knowledge base.\n- productivity:start: Initialize the productivity system and open the dashboard. Use when setting up the plugin for the first time, bootstrapping working memory from your existing task list, or decoding the shorthand (nicknames, acronyms, project codenames) you use in your todos.\n- productivity:task-management: Simple task management using a shared TASKS.md file. Reference this when the user asks about their tasks, wants to add/complete tasks, or needs help tracking commitments.\n- productivity:update: Sync tasks and refresh memory from your current activity. Use when pulling new assignments from your project tracker into TASKS.md, triaging stale or overdue tasks, filling memory gaps for unknown people or projects, or running a comprehensive scan to catch todos buried in chat and email.\n- cowork-plugin-management:cowork-plugin-customizer: Customize a Claude Code plugin for a specific organization's tools and workflows. Use when: customize plugin, set up plugin, configure plugin, tailor plugin, adjust plugin settings, customize plugin connectors, customize plugin skill, tweak plugin, modify plugin configuration.\n- cowork-plugin-management:create-cowork-plugin: Guide users through creating a new plugin from scratch in a cowork session. Use when users want to create a plugin, build a plugin, make a new plugin, develop a plugin, scaffold a plugin, start a plugin from scratch, or design a plugin. This skill requires Cowork mode with access to the outputs directory for delivering the final .plugin file.\n- engineering:architecture: Create or evaluate an architecture decision record (ADR). Use when choosing between technologies (e.g., Kafka vs SQS), documenting a design decision with trade-offs and consequences, reviewing a system design proposal, or designing a new component from requirements and constraints.\n- engineering:code-review: Review code changes for security, performance, and correctness. Trigger with a PR URL or diff, \"review this before I merge\", \"is this code safe?\", or when checking a change for N+1 queries, injection risks, missing edge cases, or error handling gaps.\n- engineering:debug: Structured debugging session — reproduce, isolate, diagnose, and fix. Trigger with an error message or stack trace, \"this works in staging but not prod\", \"something broke after the deploy\", or when behavior diverges from expected and the cause isn't obvious.\n- engineering:deploy-checklist: Pre-deployment verification checklist. Use when about to ship a release, deploying a change with database migrations or feature flags, verifying CI status and approvals before going to production, or documenting rollback triggers ahead of time.\n- engineering:documentation: Write and maintain technical documentation. Trigger with \"write docs for\", \"document this\", \"create a README\", \"write a runbook\", \"onboarding guide\", or when the user needs help with any form of technical writing — API docs, architecture docs, or operational runbooks.\n- engineering:incident-response: Run an incident response workflow — triage, communicate, and write postmortem. Trigger with \"we have an incident\", \"production is down\", an alert that needs severity assessment, a status update mid-incident, or when writing a blameless postmortem after resolution.\n- engineering:standup: Generate a standup update from recent activity. Use when preparing for daily standup, summarizing yesterday's commits and PRs and ticket moves, formatting work into yesterday/today/blockers, or structuring a few rough notes into a shareable update.\n- engineering:system-design: Design systems, services, and architectures. Trigger with \"design a system for\", \"how should we architect\", \"system design for\", \"what's the right architecture for\", or when the user needs help with API design, data modeling, or service boundaries.\n- engineering:tech-debt: Identify, categorize, and prioritize technical debt. Trigger with \"tech debt\", \"technical debt audit\", \"what should we refactor\", \"code health\", or when the user asks about code quality, refactoring priorities, or maintenance backlog.\n- engineering:testing-strategy: Design test strategies and test plans. Trigger with \"how should we test\", \"test strategy for\", \"write tests for\", \"test plan\", \"what tests do we need\", or when the user needs help with testing approaches, coverage, or test architecture.\n- design:accessibility-review: Run a WCAG 2.1 AA accessibility audit on a design or page. Trigger with \"audit accessibility\", \"check a11y\", \"is this accessible?\", or when reviewing a design for color contrast, keyboard navigation, touch target size, or screen reader behavior before handoff.\n- design:design-critique: Get structured design feedback on usability, hierarchy, and consistency. Trigger with \"review this design\", \"critique this mockup\", \"what do you think of this screen?\", or when sharing a Figma link or screenshot for feedback at any stage from exploration to final polish.\n- design:design-handoff: Generate developer handoff specs from a design. Use when a design is ready for engineering and needs a spec sheet covering layout, design tokens, component props, interaction states, responsive breakpoints, edge cases, and animation details.\n- design:design-system: Audit, document, or extend your design system. Use when checking for naming inconsistencies or hardcoded values across components, writing documentation for a component's variants, states, and accessibility notes, or designing a new pattern that fits the existing system.\n- design:research-synthesis: Synthesize user research into themes, insights, and recommendations. Use when you have interview transcripts, survey results, usability test notes, support tickets, or NPS responses that need to be distilled into patterns, user segments, and prioritized next steps.\n- design:user-research: Plan, conduct, and synthesize user research. Trigger with \"user research plan\", \"interview guide\", \"usability test\", \"survey design\", \"research questions\", or when the user needs help with any aspect of understanding their users through research.\n- design:ux-copy: Write or review UX copy — microcopy, error messages, empty states, CTAs. Trigger with \"write copy for\", \"what should this button say?\", \"review this error message\", or when naming a CTA, wording a confirmation dialog, filling an empty state, or writing onboarding text.\n- anthropic-skills:consolidate-memory: Reflective pass over your memory files — merge duplicates, fix stale facts, prune the index.\n- anthropic-skills:docx: Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, edit, or manipulate Word documents (.docx files) or Word templates (.dotx files). Triggers include: any mention of 'Word doc', 'word document', '.docx', '.dotx', or requests to produce professional documents with formatting like tables of contents, headings, page numbers, or letterheads. Also use when extracting or reorganizing content from .docx or .dotx files, inserting or replacing images in documents, performing find-and-replace in Word files, working with tracked changes or comments, or converting content into a polished Word document. If the user asks for a 'report', 'memo', 'letter', 'template', or similar deliverable as a Word or .docx file, use this skill. Do NOT use for PDFs, spreadsheets, Google Docs, or general coding tasks unrelated to document generation.\n- anthropic-skills:explain-usage: Explain where this session's tokens went, with one simple chart in plain language. Use when the user says things like \"explain my usage\", \"where did my tokens go\", or asks for a usage breakdown.\n- anthropic-skills:morning: Render the user's morning brief as a styled HTML artifact, or set it up as a recurring weekday task. Use only when the user explicitly asks to run, see, or set up their morning brief, or if they invoke /morning by name. A question about their day, schedule, or calendar is not by itself a request for the brief; answer it directly instead.\n- anthropic-skills:pdf: Use this skill whenever the user wants to do anything with PDF files. This includes reading or extracting text/tables from PDFs, combining or merging multiple PDFs into one, splitting PDFs apart, rotating pages, adding watermarks, creating new PDFs, filling PDF forms, encrypting/decrypting PDFs, extracting images, and OCR on scanned PDFs to make them searchable. If the user mentions a .pdf file or asks to produce one, use this skill.\n- anthropic-skills:pptx: Use this skill any time a .pptx or .potx file is involved in any way — as input, output, or both. This includes: creating slide decks, pitch decks, or presentations; reading, parsing, or extracting text from any .pptx or .potx file (even if the extracted content will be used elsewhere, like in an email or summary); editing, modifying, or updating existing presentations; combining or splitting slide files; working with templates (.potx), layouts, speaker notes, or comments. Trigger whenever the user mentions \"deck,\" \"slides,\" \"presentation,\" or references a .pptx or .potx filename, regardless of what they plan to do with the content afterward. If a .pptx or .potx file needs to be opened, created, or touched, use this skill.\n- anthropic-skills:schedule: Create or update a scheduled task that runs automatically. Use when the user says things like \"every day\", \"each morning\", \"remind me in an hour\", \"run this at noon\", or wants to reschedule an existing task.\n- anthropic-skills:setup-cowork: Guided Cowork setup — install role-matched plugins, connect your tools, try a skill.\n- anthropic-skills:skill-creator: Create new skills, modify and improve existing skills, and measure skill performance. Use when users want to create a skill from scratch, edit, or optimize an existing skill, run evals to test a skill, benchmark skill performance with variance analysis, or optimize a skill's description for better triggering accuracy.\n- anthropic-skills:xlsx: Use this skill any time a spreadsheet file is the primary input or output. This means any task where the user wants to: open, read, edit, or fix an existing .xlsx, .xlsm, .xltx, .csv, or .tsv file (e.g., adding columns, computing formulas, formatting, charting, cleaning messy data); create a new spreadsheet from scratch or from other data sources; or convert between tabular file formats. Trigger especially when the user references a spreadsheet file by name or path — even casually (like \"the xlsx in my downloads\") — and wants something done to it or produced from it. Also trigger for cleaning or restructuring messy tabular data files (malformed rows, misplaced headers, junk data) into proper spreadsheets. The deliverable must be a spreadsheet file. Do NOT trigger when the primary deliverable is a Word document, HTML report, standalone Python script, database pipeline, or Google Sheets API integration, even if tabular data is involved.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-diagramming: Diagramming know-how for Artifacts — when a picture earns its place, how to draw one that shows the real mechanism, and the inline-SVG mechanics that keep it legible in both themes.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the The runner computes `basis` correctly and then never consults it — firing is `servePolicy.bandCeiling.capApplies`, a supplied boolean, and `label`/`liftPath` are one static string regardless of distribution. Only the *values* ride VG-02 (`07-build-order.md:235`: \"*values* for the tier list, mapping tables, flip row, band labels and cut points ride VG-02\"); the rule itself is RULED. The correct shape already exists two gates earlier: the composition budget measures, compares against a register-supplied `bound`, and refuses loudly when unresolved (`packages/serve/src/index.ts:246-256`). The cap should do the same — take the ordered label vocabulary and cut-point matrix as typed register inputs, derive the ceiling from `basis`, and cap when the candidate band outranks it. Related: `runServeGateChain:337-341` consumes the `BandCeilingDecision` without validating it, so blank labels/refs reach persistence and are only caught at read time by `BandCeilingSchema`; and the runner's `capApplies === true` branch has **no test at any layer** (the integration fixture pins `capApplies: false`, the CAPPED unit test stubs the dependency out) — P16/G4 requires both states exercisable by a producible configuration.\n\n3. **BLOCKING** — `packages/serve/src/index.ts:370-467`, `:475-515`, `:644`; `tools/orphan-audit/src/index.ts:211-239`. These ACs are S5's per `09-traceability.md:645`. | \"POST_COMPOSE_R9_FAIL\"\n242\t  | \"BAND_CEILING_PASS\"\n243\t  | \"BAND_CEILING_CAPPED\"\n244\t  | \"COMPONENTS_ONLY_DEFECT\"\n245\t  | \"SERVE\";\n246\t\n247\texport type AnswerForm =\n248\t  | { readonly kind: \"VERDICT\"; readonly text: string }\n249\t  | {\n250\t      readonly kind: \"HYPOTHESIS_WITH_RESEARCH_PLAN\";\n251\t      readonly hypothesis: string;\n252\t      readonly researchPlan: string;\n253\t    };\n254\t\n255\texport interface ServeGateResult {\n256\t  readonly terminal: \"SERVED\" | \"DOWNGRADED\" | \"BLOCKED\" | \"COMPONENTS_ONLY\";\n257\t  readonly conformance: readonly ConformanceJudgement[];\n262\t  readonly coverageMode: \"EXHAUSTIVE\" | \"SAMPLED\" | \"NOT_RUN\";\n263\t  readonly segments: readonly ComposedSegment[];\n264\t  readonly compositionBudget: CompositionBudgetResolution;\n265\t  readonly confidenceBand: string | null;\n266\t  readonly bandCeiling: BandCeiling | null;\n267\t  readonly projections: {\n268\t    readonly reversalPoint: string;\n269\t    readonly buildsOnPrevious: FactBundle[\"buildsOnPrevious\"];\n270\t  };\n271\t}\n272\t\n273\texport function compositionEvidenceRequired(\n274\t  result: Pick<ServeGateResult, \"terminal\" | \"coverageMode\">\n275\t): boolean {\n276\t  return !(result.terminal === \"COMPONENTS_ONLY\" && result.coverageMode === \"NOT_RUN\");\n277\t}\n278\t\n279\tfunction componentsOnly(\n280\t  input: ServeGateInput,\n281\t  gateTrace: readonly GateTrace[],\n282\t  segments: readonly ComposedSegment[],\n283\t  conformance: readonly ConformanceJudgement[],\n284\t  coverageMode: ServeGateResult[\"coverageMode\"]\n285\t): ServeGateResult {\n286\t  return {\n287\t    terminal: \"COMPONENTS_ONLY\",\n288\t    answerForm: null,\n289\t    factBundle: input.factBundle,\n290\t    gateTrace,\n291\t    conditionMarks: input.factBundle.conditionMarks.includes(\"DEFECT\")\n292\t      ? input.factBundle.conditionMarks\n293\t      : [...input.factBundle.conditionMarks, \"DEFECT\"],\n294\t    conformance,\n295\t    coverageMode,\n296\t    segments,\n297\t    compositionBudget: input.compositionBudget,\n298\t    confidenceBand: null,\n299\t    bandCeiling: null,\n300\t    projections: {\n301\t      reversalPoint: input.factBundle.reversalPoint,\n302\t      buildsOnPrevious: input.factBundle.buildsOnPrevious\n303\t    }\n304\t  };\n305\t}\n306\t\n307\texport async function runServeGateChain(\n308\t  input: ServeGateInput,\n309\t  dependencies: ServeGateDependencies\n310\t): Promise<ServeGateResult> {\n311\t  const trace: GateTrace[] = [];\n312\t\n313\t  if (input.nodes.length === 0) {\n314\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"SERVE_NODE_SET_EMPTY\", \"A serve chain requires at least one node\");\n315\t  }\n316\t  if (input.nodes.some((node) => node.loadBearing && node.restatementStatus !== \"PASS\")) {\n317\t    trace.push(\"GATE1_R9_BLOCK\", \"COMPONENTS_ONLY_DEFECT\");\n318\t    return componentsOnly(input, trace, [], [], \"NOT_RUN\");\n319\t  }\n320\t  trace.push(\"GATE1_R9_PASS\");\n321\t\n322\t  if (input.factBundle.residualObjections.length > 0) {\n323\t    trace.push(\"GATE2_Q53_BLOCK\", \"COMPONENTS_ONLY_DEFECT\");\n324\t    return componentsOnly(input, trace, [], [], \"NOT_RUN\");\n325\t  }\n326\t  trace.push(\"GATE2_Q53_PASS_VACUOUS\");\n327\t\n328\t  if (input.maxRecompose !== 2) {\n329\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"MAX_RECOMPOSE_INVALID\", \"DR-049 requires max_recompose = 2\");\n330\t  }\n331\t  if (!Number.isFinite(input.compositionBudget.bound) || input.compositionBudget.bound < 0) {\n332\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"COMPOSITION_BUDGET_UNRESOLVED\", \"A V-ratified composition budget is required\");\n333\t  }\n334\t  const measuredBundle = dependencies.measureCompositionBundle(input.factBundle);\n335\t  if (!Number.isFinite(measuredBundle) || measuredBundle < 0) {\n336\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"COMPOSITION_MEASUREMENT_INVALID\", \"The composition-bundle measurement must be finite\");\n337\t  }\n338\t  if (measuredBundle > input.compositionBudget.bound) {\n339\t    trace.push(\"COMPOSITION_BUDGET_EXCEEDED\", \"COMPONENTS_ONLY_DEFECT\");\n340\t    return componentsOnly(input, trace, [], [], \"NOT_RUN\");\n341\t  }\n342\t  trace.push(\"COMPOSITION_BUDGET_PASS\");\n343\t\n344\t  let segments: readonly ComposedSegment[] = [];\n345\t  let conformance: readonly ConformanceJudgement[] = [];\n346\t  let coverageMode: ServeGateResult[\"coverageMode\"] = \"NOT_RUN\";\n347\t  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= input.maxRecompose; attempt += 1) {\n348\t    const composed = await dependencies.compose(input.factBundle, attempt);\n349\t    const nodeIds = new Set(input.nodes.map((node) => node.nodeId));\n350\t    const loadBearingNodeIds = new Set(input.nodes.filter((node) => node.loadBearing).map((node) => node.nodeId));\n351\t    if (composed.some((segment) => segment.assertedNodeRefs.some((nodeRef) => !nodeIds.has(nodeRef)))) {\n352\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\"COMPOSITION_CONTRACT_ERROR\", \"A composed segment references a node outside the serve set\");\n353\t    }\n354\t    segments = composed.map((segment) => Object.freeze({\n355\t      ...segment,\n356\t      loadBearing: segment.servedNumberRefs.length > 0\n357\t        || segment.assertedNodeRefs.some((nodeRef) => loadBearingNodeIds.has(nodeRef))\n358\t    }));\n359\t    if (segments.length === 0 || segments.some((segment) =>\n360\t      segment.segmentId.trim().length === 0 || segment.text.trim().length === 0\n361\t    )) {\n362\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\"COMPOSITION_CONTRACT_ERROR\", \"Composition returned no segments\");\n363\t    }\n364\t    if (new Set(segments.map((segment) => segment.segmentId)).size !== segments.length) {\n365\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\"COMPOSITION_CONTRACT_ERROR\", \"Composed segment ids must be stable and unique\");\n366\t    }\n367\t    trace.push(attempt === 1 ? \"COMPOSED\" : \"RECOMPOSED_ONCE\");\n368\t    coverageMode = input.strangerSampleRate >= 1 ? \"EXHAUSTIVE\" : \"SAMPLED\";\n369\t    conformance = await Promise.all(segments.map(async (segment) => {\n370\t      if (segment.loadBearing) return dependencies.conform(segment, \"JUDGED\");\n371\t      if (input.strangerSampleRate >= 1 || dependencies.selectSample(segment, input.strangerSampleRate)) {\n372\t        return dependencies.conform(segment, \"SAMPLED_PASSED\");\n373\t      }\n374\t      return { segmentId: segment.segmentId, state: \"NOT_SAMPLED\", conforms: true } as const;\n375\t    }));\n376\t    if (conformance.every((judgement) => judgement.conforms)) {\n377\t      trace.push(coverageMode === \"EXHAUSTIVE\"\n378\t        ? \"GATE3_CONFORMANCE_PASS_EXHAUSTIVE\"\n379\t        : \"GATE3_CONFORMANCE_PASS_SAMPLED\");\n380\t      break;\n381\t    }\n382\t    trace.push(\"GATE3_CONFORMANCE_FAIL\");\n383\t  }\n384\t\n385\t  if (!conformance.every((judgement) => judgement.conforms)) {\n386\t    trace.push(\"COMPONENTS_ONLY_DEFECT\");\n387\t    return componentsOnly(input, trace, segments, conformance, coverageMode);\n388\t  }\n389\t\n390\t  let terminal: ServeGateResult[\"terminal\"];\n391\t  let answerForm: AnswerForm;\n392\t  const loadBearingNodes = input.nodes.filter((node) => node.loadBearing);\n393\t  if (loadBearingNodes.some((node) => node.wayOfKnowing === \"LOOKED_UP\" && node.locator === null)) {\n394\t    trace.push(\"GATE4_Q51_LOCATOR_BLOCK\", \"COMPONENTS_ONLY_DEFECT\");\n395\t    return componentsOnly(input, trace, segments, conformance, coverageMode);\n396\t  }\n397\t  if (loadBearingNodes.every((node) => node.wayOfKnowing === \"REASONING\")) {\n398\t    if (segments.length < 2 || segments[0] === undefined || segments[1] === undefined) {\n399\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\n400\t        \"COMPOSITION_CONTRACT_ERROR\",\n401\t        \"A reasoning answer requires both a hypothesis and a research-plan segment\"\n402\t      );\n403\t    }\n404\t    trace.push(\"GATE4_Q51_DOWNGRADE\");\n405\t    terminal = \"DOWNGRADED\";\n406\t    answerForm = {\n407\t      kind: \"HYPOTHESIS_WITH_RESEARCH_PLAN\",\n408\t      hypothesis: segments[0].text,\n409\t      researchPlan: segments[1].text\n410\t    };\n411\t  } else {\n412\t    trace.push(\"GATE4_Q51_PASS\");\n413\t    terminal = \"SERVED\";\n414\t    answerForm = { kind: \"VERDICT\", text: segments.map((segment) => segment.text).join(\"\\n\") };\n415\t  }\n416\t\n417\t  // DR-129 ratifies Q51 before the post-compose verdict-R9 limb; keep this citation at the chain.\n418\t  if (!await dependencies.postComposeR9(segments)) {\n419\t    trace.push(\"POST_COMPOSE_R9_FAIL\", \"COMPONENTS_ONLY_DEFECT\");\n420\t    return componentsOnly(input, trace, segments, conformance, coverageMode);\n421\t  }\n422\t  trace.push(\"POST_COMPOSE_R9_PASS\");\n423\t  const loadBearing = input.nodes.filter((node) => node.loadBearing);\n424\t  const basis = {\n425\t    LOOKED_UP: loadBearing.filter((node) => node.wayOfKnowing === \"LOOKED_UP\").length,\n426\t    RAN: loadBearing.filter((node) => node.wayOfKnowing === \"RAN\").length,\n427\t    REASONING: loadBearing.filter((node) => node.wayOfKnowing === \"REASONING\").length\n428\t  };\n429\t  const ceilingDecision = dependencies.applyBandCeiling({\n430\t    basis,\n431\t    candidateConfidenceBand: input.candidateConfidenceBand\n432\t  });\n433\t  validateBandCeilingDecision(ceilingDecision, input.candidateConfidenceBand, basis);\n434\t  trace.push(ceilingDecision.kind === \"CAPPED\" ? \"BAND_CEILING_CAPPED\" : \"BAND_CEILING_PASS\", \"SERVE\");\n435\t  return {\n436\t    terminal,\n437\t    answerForm,\n438\t    factBundle: input.factBundle,\n439\t    gateTrace: trace,\n440\t    conditionMarks: input.factBundle.conditionMarks,\n441\t    conformance,\n442\t    coverageMode,\n443\t    segments,\n444\t    compositionBudget: input.compositionBudget,\n445\t    confidenceBand: ceilingDecision.confidenceBand,\n446\t    bandCeiling: ceilingDecision.ceiling,\n447\t    projections: {\n448\t      reversalPoint: input.factBundle.reversalPoint,\n449\t      buildsOnPrevious: input.factBundle.buildsOnPrevious\n450\t    }\n451\t  };\n452\t}\n453\t\n454\tconst SERVE_ITEM_STATUSES = [\"READY\", \"PENDING\", \"ERROR\"] as const;\n455\ttype ServeItemStatus = typeof SERVE_ITEM_STATUSES[number];\n456\t\n457\texport interface ServeItem {\n458\t  readonly nodeId: string;\n459\t  readonly status: ServeItemStatus;\n460\t  readonly reason?: string | null;\n461\t}\n462\t\n463\texport function validateServeItems(input: {\n464\t  readonly ledgerProduced: boolean;\n465\t  readonly items: unknown;\n466\t  readonly currentNodeIds: readonly string[];\n467\t}): readonly ServeItem[] {\n468\t  if (!input.ledgerProduced) {\n469\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"SERVE_OUTPUT_NOT_FROM_LEDGER\", \"Serve output must be ledger-produced\");\n470\t  }\n471\t  if (!Array.isArray(input.items)) {\n472\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"SERVE_ITEMS_NOT_A_LIST\", \"Serve items must be a list\");\n473\t  }\n474\t  for (const item of input.items) {\n475\t    if (typeof item !== \"object\" || item === null || !(\"nodeId\" in item) || !(\"status\" in item)\n476\t      || typeof item.nodeId !== \"string\" || typeof item.status !== \"string\") {\n477\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\"SERVE_ITEM_INVALID\", \"Every serve item must validate\");\n478\t    }\n479\t    if (!(SERVE_ITEM_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(item.status)) {\n480\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\"SERVE_STATUS_UNKNOWN\", `Unknown serve status: ${item.status}`);\n481\t    }\n482\t    if (!input.currentNodeIds.includes(item.nodeId)) {\n483\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\"SERVE_ITEM_OUT_OF_NODE_SET\", `Node ${item.nodeId} is not current`);\n484\t    }\n485\t  }\n486\t  return input.items as readonly ServeItem[];\n487\t}\n488\t\n489\tconst SECRET_MARKER = /(?:authorization|bearer|api[_-]?key|token|secret)/i;\n490\t\n491\texport function sanitizeServeItem(input: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>): Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> {\n492\t  const debug = typeof input.debug === \"object\" && input.debug !== null && \"contractVersion\" in input.debug\n493\t    && typeof input.debug.contractVersion === \"string\"\n494\t    ? { contractVersion: input.debug.contractVersion }\n495\t    : undefined;\n496\t  const reason = typeof input.reason === \"string\" && !SECRET_MARKER.test(input.reason) ? input.reason : null;\n497\t  return Object.freeze({\n498\t    nodeId: input.nodeId,\n499\t    status: input.status,\n500\t    ...(debug === undefined ? {} : { debug }),\n501\t    ...(Object.hasOwn(input, \"reason\") ? { reason } : {})\n502\t  });\n503\t}\n504\t\n505\texport function reconcileServeItems(input: {\n506\t  readonly currentNodes: readonly { readonly nodeId: string; readonly workActive: boolean }[];\n507\t  readonly items: readonly ServeItem[];\n508\t}): readonly ServeItem[] {\n509\t  const byNode = new Map(input.items.map((item) => [item.nodeId, item]));\n510\t  return input.currentNodes.map((node) => byNode.get(node.nodeId) ?? (node.workActive\n511\t    ? { nodeId: node.nodeId, status: \"PENDING\" as const }\n512\t    : { nodeId: node.nodeId, status: \"ERROR\" as const, reason: \"MISSING_COMPLETED_ITEM\" }));\n513\t}\n514\t\n515\texport function deriveWorkReadState(input: {\n516\t  readonly storedState: \"ACTIVE\" | \"DONE\" | \"FAILED\";\n517\t  readonly deadline: Date | null;\n518\t  readonly readAt: Date;\n519\t}): { readonly state: \"ACTIVE\" | \"DONE\" | \"FAILED\"; readonly reason?: \"DEADLINE_EXPIRED\" } {\n520\t  return input.storedState === \"ACTIVE\" && input.deadline !== null && input.readAt > input.deadline\n521\t    ? { state: \"FAILED\", reason: \"DEADLINE_EXPIRED\" }\n522\t    : { state: input.storedState };\n523\t}\n524\t\n525\texport function deriveHonestVerdict(input: verdictState: null; readonly confidenceBand: null; readonly unavailable: { readonly reasonRef: string } } {\n528\t  return input.usableBasis\n529\t    ? { verdictState: \"SUPPORTED\", confidenceBand: null, unavailable: null }\n530\t    : { verdictState: null, confidenceBand: null, unavailable: { reasonRef: input.reasonRef } };\n531\t}\n532\t\n533\texport function projectProvenance(input: {\n534\t  readonly sourceRef: string;\n535\t  readonly producer: string;\n536\t  readonly replayHandle: string;\n537\t  readonly perSide: {\n538\t    readonly support: readonly string[];\n539\t    readonly attack: readonly string[];\n540\t  };\n541\t}, flip: {\n542\t  readonly layer2Enabled: boolean;\n543\t  readonly registerRowKey: string;\n544\t  readonly registerVersion: number;\n545\t  readonly sourceRef: string;\n546\t}): Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> {\n547\t  const layering = Object.freeze({\n548\t    registerRowKey: flip.registerRowKey,\n549\t    registerVersion: flip.registerVersion,\n550\t    sourceRef: flip.sourceRef,\n551\t    layer: flip.layer2Enabled ? 2 : 1\n552\t  });\n553\t  return Object.freeze({\n554\t    sourceRef: input.sourceRef,\n555\t    producer: input.producer,\n556\t    replayHandle: input.replayHandle,\n557\t    ...(flip.layer2Enabled ? { perSide: input.perSide } : {}),\n558\t    layering\n559\t  });\n560\t}\n561\t\n562\texport interface ServedNumberEvent {\n563\t  readonly status: \"PRESENT\" | \"EVICTED\" | \"WITHHELD\";\n564\t  readonly reason: string | null;\n565\t  readonly atSequence: number;\n566\t}\n567\t\n568\texport function foldServedNumberEvents(events: readonly ServedNumberEvent[], throughSequence?: number): {\n569\t  readonly status: ServedNumberEvent[\"status\"];\n570\t  readonly reason: string | null;\n571\t} {\n572\t  const latest = events\n573\t    .filter((event) => throughSequence === undefined || event.atSequence <= throughSequence)\n574\t    .sort((left, right) => left.atSequence - right.atSequence)\n575\t    .at(-1);\n576\t  if (latest === undefined) {\n577\t    throw new TypedDomainError(\"EMPTY_EVENT_STREAM\", \"A served number must have at least one status event\");\n578\t  }\n579\t  return { status: latest.status, reason: latest.reason };\n580\t}\n581\t\n582\texport function deriveAnswerServeState(input: {\n583\t  readonly sealedServeState: \"COMPOSED\" | \"RECOMPOSED_ONCE\" | \"COMPONENTS_ONLY\";\n584\t  readonly numberEvents: readonly ServedNumberEvent[];\n585\t  readonly readMode: \"CURRENT\" | \"SEALED_VERSION\";\n586\t}): {\n587\t  readonly serveState: \"COMPOSED\" | \"RECOMPOSED_ONCE\" | \"COMPONENTS_ONLY\";\n588\t  readonly conditionMarks: readonly \"DEFECT\"[];\n589\t} {\n590\t  if (input.readMode === \"SEALED_VERSION\") evicted\n595\t    ? { serveState: \"COMPONENTS_ONLY\", conditionMarks: [\"DEFECT\"] }\n596\t    : { serveState: input.sealedServeState, conditionMarks: [] };\n597\t}\n598\t\n599\texport interface ReplaySelfTestInput {\n600\t  readonly stored: number;\n601\t  readonly recomputed: number;\n602\t  readonly servedNumberId: string;\n603\t}\n604\t\n605\texport function decideReplayEviction(input: ReplaySelfTestInput):\n606\t  | { readonly kind: \"UNCHANGED\" }\n607\t  | { readonly kind: \"EVICT\"; readonly servedNumberId: string; readonly mark: \"MISSING-NUMBER\" } {\n608\t  return Object.is(input.stored, input.recomputed)\n609\t    ? { kind: \"UNCHANGED\" }\n610\t    : { kind: \"EVICT\", servedNumberId: input.servedNumberId, mark: \"MISSING-NUMBER\" };\n611\t}\n612\t\n613\texport interface PersistServeInput {\n614\t  readonly runId: string;\n615\t  readonly workItemId: string;\n616\t  readonly factBundleVersion: number;\n617\t  readonly factBundleContentHash: string;\n618\t  readonly factBundle: FactBundle;\n619\t  readonly result: ServeGateResult;\n620\t  readonly segments: readonly ComposedSegment[];\n621\t  readonly compositionRawArtifactRef: string | null;\n622\t  readonly compositionAttempt: number;\n623\t  readonly conformanceRawArtifactRefs: readonly string[];\n624\t  readonly servedNumber: {\n625\t    readonly numberRef: string;\n626\t    readonly value: number;\n627\t    readonly numberKind: string;\n628\t    readonly sourceRef: string;\n629\t    readonly producer: string;\n630\t    readonly replayHandle: string;\n631\t    readonly propagationRunId: string;\n632\t  } | null;\n633\t}\n634\t\n635\texport class ServeRepository {\n636\t  constructor(private readonly pool: Pool) {}\n637\t\n638\t  async recordReplayEviction(servedNumberId: string): Promise<void> {\n639\t    await withWriteTransaction(this.pool, async (client) => {\n640\t      const number = await client.query<{\n641\t        answer_id: string;\n642\t        answer_version: number;\n643\t        number_ref: string;\n644\t        segments: Array<{ segment_id: string; served_number_refs: string[] }> | null;\n645\t      }>(\n646\t        `SELECT number.answer_id, number.answer_version, number.number_ref, composed.segments\n647\t         FROM serve.served_number AS number\n648\t         JOIN serve.answer AS answer\n649\t           ON answer.answer_id = number.answer_id AND answer.answer_version = number.answer_version\n650\t         LEFT JOIN serve.composed_text AS composed ON composed.composed_text_id = answer.composed_text_id\n651\t         WHERE number.served_number_id = $1`,\n652\t        [servedNumberId]\n653\t      );\n654\t      const owner = number.rows[0];\n655\t      if (owner === undefined) {\n656\t        throw new TypedDomainError(\"SERVED_NUMBER_NOT_FOUND\", `No served number ${servedNumberId} exists`);\n657\t      }\n658\t      await client.query(\n659\t        `INSERT INTO serve.served_number_event (served_number_id, status, reason, at_seq)\n660\t         VALUES ($1,'EVICTED','MISSING-NUMBER',$2)`,\n661\t        [servedNumberId, await allocateSequence(client)]\n662\t      );\n663\t      for (const segment of owner.segments ?? []) {\n664\t        if (!segment.served_number_refs.includes(owner.number_ref)) continue;\n665\t        await client.query(\n666\t          `INSERT INTO serve.segment_suppression (\n667\t             answer_id, answer_version, segment_id, evicted_number_ref, at_seq\n668\t           ) VALUES ($1,$2,$3,$4,$5)\n669\t           ON CONFLICT (answer_id, answer_version, segment_id, evicted_number_ref) DO NOTHING`,\n670\t          [owner.answer_id, owner.answer_version, segment.segment_id, owner.number_ref, await allocateSequence(client)]\n671\t        );\n672\t      }\n673\t    });\n674\t  }\n675\t\n676\t  async persist(input: PersistServeInput): Promise<{ readonly answerId: string; readonly servedNumberId: string | null }> {\n677\t    if (input.result.terminal === \"BLOCKED\") {\n678\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\n679\t        \"BLOCKED_TERMINAL_RETIRED\",\n680\t        \"DR-130 routes pre-compose blocking gates to COMPONENTS_ONLY with DEFECT\"\n681\t      );\n682\t    }\n683\t    if (input.compositionRawArtifactRef === null && compositionEvidenceRequired(input.result)) {\n684\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\n685\t        \"MISSING_COMPOSITION_ARTIFACT\",\n686\t        \"Only a pre-composition terminal may omit composition evidence\"\n687\t      );\n688\t    }\n689\t    if (input.compositionRawArtifactRef === null && (\n690\t      input.compositionAttempt !== 0\n691\t      || input.segments.length !== 0\n692\t      || input.conformanceRawArtifactRefs.length !== 0\n693\t      || input.result.conformance.length !== 0\n694\t    )) {\n695\t      throw new }\n700\t    if (input.compositionRawArtifactRef !== null && input.compositionAttempt < 1) {\n701\t      throw new TypedDomainError(\"INVALID_COMPOSITION_ATTEMPT\", \"A composition artifact requires a input.result.terminal === \"DOWNGRADED\"),\n706\t      reasonRef: `serve-gate:${input.result.gateTrace.at(-1) ?? input.result.terminal}`\n707\t    });\n708\t    return withWriteTransaction(this.pool, answer.answer_version\n33\t  AND answer.sealed_at_seq < initial_event.at_seq;\n34\t\n35\tALTER TABLE serve.answer\n36\t  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS answer_serve_state_check,\n37\t  ADD CONSTRAINT S05 serve pipeline hardening: sealed artifacts plus append-only read overlays.\n2\t\n3\t-- Forward-apply S04 review parity for databases that already recorded 0005.\n4\tCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION register.claim_type_composition_map_is_valid(candidate jsonb)\n5\tRETURNS boolean\n6\tLANGUAGE plpgsql\n7\tIMMUTABLE\n8\tPARALLEL SAFE\n9\tAS $$\n10\tDECLARE\n11\t  member_key text;\n12\t  member jsonb;\n13\t  item jsonb;\n14\tBEGIN\n15\t  IF jsonb_typeof(candidate) <> 'object'\n16\t    OR candidate ->> 'kind' <> 'CLAIM_TYPE_COMPOSITION_MAP'\n17\t    OR jsonb_typeof(candidate -> 'entries') <> 'object'\n18\t    OR candidate - ARRAY['kind', 'entries']::text[] <> '{}'::jsonb\n19\t  THEN\n20\t    RETURN false;\n21\t  END IF;\n22\t\n23\t  FOR member_key, member IN SELECT key, value FROM jsonb_each(candidate -> 'entries')\n24\t  LOOP\n25\t    IF member_key NOT IN ('empirical', 'causal', 'normative', 'definitional', 'prediction', 'comparative', 'mixed', 'unknown')\n26\t      OR jsonb_typeof(member) <> 'object'\n27\t      OR NOT member ?& ARRAY['branch', 'clarityDecayPerAmbiguity', 'terms', 'caps', 'uncertaintyLadder']\n28\t      OR member - ARRAY['branch', 'clarityDecayPerAmbiguity', 'terms', 'caps', 'uncertaintyLadder']::text[] <> '{}'::jsonb\n29\t      OR member ->> 'branch' NOT IN ('EVIDENCE_AWARE', 'EVIDENCE_FREE')\n30\t      OR jsonb_typeof(member -> 'clarityDecayPerAmbiguity') <> 'number'\n31\t      OR (member ->> 'clarityDecayPerAmbiguity')::numeric NOT BETWEEN 0 AND 1\n32\t      OR jsonb_typeof(member -> 'terms') <> 'array'\n33\t      OR jsonb_typeof(member -> 'caps') <> 'array'\n34\t      OR jsonb_typeof(member -> 'uncertaintyLadder') <> 'array'\n35\t    THEN\n36\t      RETURN false;\n37\t    END IF;\n38\t\n39\t    FOR item IN SELECT value FROM jsonb_array_elements(member -> 'terms')\n40\t    LOOP\n41\t      IF jsonb_typeof(item) <> 'object'\n42\t        OR NOT item ?& ARRAY['metric', 'coefficient']\n43\t        OR item - ARRAY['metric', 'coefficient']::text[] <> '{}'::jsonb\n44\t        OR item ->> 'metric' NOT IN ('steelman_fidelity', 'counter_resilience', 'evidence_quality', 'evidence_relevance', 'context_fit', 'clarity', 'fallacy_resilience')\n45\t        OR jsonb_typeof(item -> 'coefficient') <> 'number'\n46\t        OR (item ->> 'coefficient')::numeric NOT BETWEEN 0 AND 1\n47\t      THEN\n48\t        RETURN false;\n49\t      END IF;\n50\t    END LOOP;\n51\t\n52\t    FOR item IN SELECT value FROM jsonb_array_elements(member -> 'caps')\n53\t    LOOP\n54\t      IF jsonb_typeof(item) <> 'object'\n55\t        OR NOT item ?& ARRAY['whenFatalType', 'to', 'why', 'by']\n56\t        OR item - ARRAY['whenFatalType', 'to', 'why', 'by']::text[] <> '{}'::jsonb\n57\t        OR jsonb_typeof(item -> 'whenFatalType') <> 'string'\n58\t        OR jsonb_typeof(item -> 'to') <> 'number'\n59\t        OR jsonb_typeof(item -> 'why') <> 'string'\n60\t        OR jsonb_typeof(item -> 'by') <> 'string'\n61\t        OR length(btrim(item ->> 'whenFatalType')) = 0\n62\t        OR (item ->> 'to')::numeric NOT BETWEEN 0 AND 1\n63\t        OR length(btrim(item ->> 'why')) = 0\n64\t        OR length(btrim(item ->> 'by')) = 0\n65\t      THEN\n66\t        RETURN false;\n67\t      END IF;\n68\t    END LOOP;\n69\t\n70\t    FOR item IN SELECT value FROM jsonb_array_elements(member -> 'uncertaintyLadder')\n71\t    LOOP\n72\t      IF jsonb_typeof(item) <> 'object'\n73\t        OR NOT item ?& ARRAY['atMost', 'label']\n74\t        OR item - ARRAY['atMost', 'label']::text[] <> '{}'::jsonb\n75\t        OR jsonb_typeof(item -> 'atMost') <> 'number'\n76\t        OR jsonb_typeof(item -> 'label') <> 'string'\n77\t        OR (item ->> 'atMost')::numeric NOT BETWEEN 0 AND 1\n78\t        OR length(btrim(item ->> 'label')) = 0\n79\t      THEN\n80\t        RETURN false;\n81\t      END IF;\n82\t    END LOOP;\n83\t  END LOOP;\n84\t  RETURN true;\n85\tEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n86\t  RETURN false;\n87\tEND;\n88\t$$;\n89\t\n90\tALTER TABLE register.register_row\n91\t  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS register_row_claim_type_composition_map_shape,\n92\t  ADD CONSTRAINT register_row_claim_type_composition_map_shape CHECK (\n93\t    row_key <> 'claimTypeCompositionMap'\n94\t    OR register.claim_type_composition_map_is_valid(value_json)\n95\t  );\n96\t\n97\tALTER TABLE serve.answer\n98\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS confidence_band text,\n99\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS band_ceiling jsonb,\n100\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reversal_point text,\n101\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS builds_on_previous jsonb,\n102\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS badges jsonb,\n103\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS verdict_unavailable jsonb;\n104\t\n105\tALTER TABLE serve.answer\n106\t  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS answer_band_ceiling_pair,\n107\t  ADD CONSTRAINT answer_band_ceiling_pair CHECK (\n108\t    (confidence_band IS NULL) = (band_ceiling IS NULL)\n109\t  ) NOT VALID,\n110\t  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS answer_verdict_projection_pair,\n111\t  ADD CONSTRAINT answer_verdict_projection_pair CHECK (\n112\t    (verdict_state IS NULL) <> (verdict_unavailable IS NULL)\n113\t    AND (verdict_unavailable IS NULL OR confidence_band IS NULL)\n114\t  ) NOT VALID;\n115\t\n116\tALTER TABLE serve.served_number\n117\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS answer_id uuid,\n118\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS answer_version integer,\n119\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS number_ref text;\n120\t\n121\tALTER TABLE serve.served_number_event\n122\t  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reason text;\n123\t\n124\tCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION serve.conformance_segment_results_are_valid(candidate jsonb)\n125\tRETURNS boolean\n126\tLANGUAGE plpgsql\n127\tIMMUTABLE\n128\tPARALLEL SAFE\n129\tAS $$\n130\tBEGIN\n131\t  IF jsonb_typeof(candidate) <> 'array' THEN\n132\t    RETURN false;\n133\t  END IF;\n134\t  RETURN NOT EXISTS (\n135\t    SELECT 1\n136\t    FROM jsonb_array_elements(candidate) AS segment\n137\t    WHERE jsonb_typeof(segment) <> 'object'\n138\t       OR segment ->> 'state' NOT IN ('JUDGED', 'SAMPLED_PASSED', 'NOT_SAMPLED')\n139\t       OR jsonb_typeof(segment -> 'segmentId') <> 'string'\n140\t       OR jsonb_typeof(segment -> 'conforms') <> 'boolean'\n141\t  );\n142\tEXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n143\t  RETURN false;\n144\tEND;\n145\t$$;\n146\t\n147\tALTER TABLE serve.served_number_event\n148\t  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS served_number_event_reason_matches_status,\n149\t  ADD CONSTRAINT served_number_event_reason_matches_status CHECK (\n150\t    (status = 'PRESENT' AND reason IS NULL)\n151\t    OR (status = 'EVICTED' AND reason = 'MISSING-NUMBER')\n152\t    OR (status = 'WITHHELD' AND reason = 'STRICT_AND_CONJUNCT_UNJUDGED_OR_ABSTAINED')\n153\t  ) NOT VALID;\n154\t\n155\tALTER TABLE serve.conformance_record\n156\t  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS conformance_record_segment_result_shape,\n157\t  ADD CONSTRAINT conformance_record_segment_result_shape\n158\t    CHECK (serve.conformance_segment_results_are_valid(segment_results)) NOT VALID;\n159\t\n160\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS serve.segment_suppression (\n161\t  answer_id uuid NOT NULL,\n162\t  answer_version integer NOT NULL,\n163\t  segment_id text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(segment_id)) > 0),\n164\t  evicted_number_ref text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(evicted_number_ref)) > 0),\n165\t  at_seq bigint NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (at_seq > 0),\n166\t  PRIMARY KEY (answer_id, answer_version, segment_id, evicted_number_ref),\n167\t  FOREIGN KEY (answer_id, answer_version)\n168\t    REFERENCES serve.answer(answer_id, answer_version)\n169\t);\n170\t\n171\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS serve.condition_mark (\n172\t  condition_mark_id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n173\t  answer_id uuid NOT NULL,\n174\t  answer_version integer NOT NULL,\n175\t  mark text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(mark)) > 0),\n176\t  scope text NOT NULL CHECK (scope IN ('answer', 'node')),\n177\t  subject_ref text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(subject_ref)) > 0),\n178\t  reason text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(reason)) > 0),\n179\t  lift_path text,\n180\t  at_seq bigint NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (at_seq > 0),\n181\t  FOREIGN KEY (answer_id, answer_version)\n182\t    REFERENCES serve.answer(answer_id, answer_version)\n183\t);\n184\t\n185\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS serve.condition_mark_node (\n186\t  condition_mark_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES serve.condition_mark(condition_mark_id),\n187\t  node_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES core.node(node_id),\n188\t  PRIMARY KEY (condition_mark_id, node_id)\n189\t);\n190\t\n191\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS serve.shadow_suppression (\n192\t  shadow_suppression_id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n193\t  answer_id uuid NOT NULL,\n194\t  answer_version integer NOT NULL,\n195\t  gate text NOT NULL CHECK (gate IN ('EVIDENCE_GATE', 'VALUE_OVERLAY')),\n196\t  subject_ref text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(subject_ref)) > 0),\n197\t  would_have_suppressed jsonb NOT NULL,\n198\t  unlock_condition text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(unlock_condition)) > 0),\n199\t  at_seq bigint NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (at_seq > 0),\n200\t  FOREIGN KEY (answer_id, answer_version)\n201\t    REFERENCES serve.answer(answer_id, answer_version)\n202\t);\n203\t\n204\tCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS serve.abstention (\n205\t  abstention_id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n206\t  answer_id uuid NOT NULL,\n207\t  answer_version integer NOT NULL,\n208\t  kind text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(kind)) > 0),\n209\t  question_class text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(question_class)) > 0),\n210\t  risk_tier text NOT NULL CHECK (risk_tier IN ('casual', 'standard', 'high-stakes')),\n211\t  price double precision NOT NULL CHECK (price > 0 AND price < 1),\n212\t  register_row_key text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(register_row_key)) > 0),\n213\t  register_version bigint NOT NULL CHECK (register_version > 0),\n214\t  register_source_ref text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(register_source_ref)) > 0),\n215\t  unlock_condition text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(unlock_condition)) > 0),\n216\t  ledger_unknown_ref text NOT NULL CHECK (length(btrim(ledger_unknown_ref)) > 0),\n217\t  at_seq bigint NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK (at_seq > 0),\n218\t  FOREIGN KEY (answer_id, answer_version)\n219\t    REFERENCES serve.answer(answer_id, answer_version)\n220\t);\n221\t\n222\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS segment_suppression_answer_version\n223\t  ON serve.segment_suppression(answer_id, answer_version, at_seq);\n224\tCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS shadow_suppression_answer_version\n225\t  ON serve.shadow_suppression(answer_id, answer_version, at_seq);\n226\t\n227\tGRANT SELECT, INSERT ON serve.segment_suppression, serve.condition_mark,\n228\t  serve.condition_mark_node, serve.shadow_suppression, serve.abstention TO debateai_runtime;\n229\tGRANT SELECT ON serve.segment_suppression, serve.condition_mark,\n230\t  serve.condition_mark_node, serve.shadow_suppression, serve.abstention TO debateai_replay;\n231\tREVOKE UPDATE, DELETE ON serve.segment_suppression, serve.condition_mark,\n232\t  serve.condition_mark_node, serve.shadow_suppression, serve.abstention FROM PUBLIC, debateai_runtime;\n233\t\n234\tDO $$\n235\tDECLARE table_name text;\n236\tBEGIN\n237\t  FOREACH table_name IN ARRAY ARRAY[\n238\t    'serve.segment_suppression', 'serve.condition_mark',\n239\t    'serve.condition_mark_node', 'serve.shadow_suppression', 'serve.abstention'\n240\t  ] LOOP\n241\t    EXECUTE format('DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS reject_mutation ON %s', table_name);\n242\t    EXECUTE format(\n243\t      'CREATE TRIGGER reject_mutation BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON %s FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION core.reject_mutation()',\n244\t      table_name\n245\t    );\n246\t  END LOOP;\n247\tEND;\n248\t$$;\n249\t"}]},"uuid":"f66d50a5-1ae8-4190-abe3-a63ef5dfcf0e","timestamp":"2026-08-08T14:52:21.832Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e561e556-f4a6-4c04-a477-e35ef1c05f2e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-desktop","cwd":"/Users/vladmihaimiron/Documents/DebateAI-V3","sessionId":"a8fcf2e3-bdd1-4a10-bcd6-3ec887012907","version":"2.1.221","gitBranch":"main","slug":"hidden-riding-bengio"}

{"parentUuid":"bd9acfa9-c9f5-49b0-8de5-05be68ed4ed3","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"af226031d0bcc0f86","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdqUZFF9zABvHj8twX9XF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"I'm confirming that `resolveSession` still has an OPERATOR scope parameter defined as a default, but no API route actually invokes it with that scope—the inspection endpoint only uses the asker token. This means the OPERATOR scope is unreachable dead code in the API layer, and the operator references in the replay app are just part of the read-only ceremony, so there's no actual operator-scope vulnerability here.\n\nThe BLOCKED terminal state only appears in the type union and the persist guard that explicitly rejects it—there's no live code constructing it anywhere. It's inert code, a minor cleanliness issue but not a security concern since DR-130 is scoped to serve_state and the guard defensively blocks it anyway.\n\nI've now verified everything needed for the verdict: C1 is fixed because the inspection route uses the asker token, passes the session to readInspection, the SQL query scopes by asker_id just like the answer projection does, the pinning test is corrected, and the real-PG cross-asker proof confirms that unauthorized access returns null.\n\nC2 is fixed because deriveBandCeiling computes the ceiling from the WOK basis with typed register inputs, the runner wires it correctly with BandCeilingRegisterRow and refuses loudly on unresolved policy, and both cap states are tested—the pure deriveBandCeiling path and the end-to-end runner path with both NOT_CAPPED and CAPPED scenarios passing.\n\nFor C3, I've attached three functions—deriveHonestVerdict, foldServedNumberEvents, and deriveAnswerServeState—while five others remain unattached and are tracked in the orphan-audit report, pinned by the scaffold test with exact-order assertions. The verdict_state is derived via deriveHonestVerdict at persist and stays null on components-only paths rather than defaulting to \"SUPPORTED\", which real Postgres confirms.\n\nFor the COMPONENTS_ONLY budget fix, I've set compositionEvidenceRequired to false for that state when NOT_RUN, allowing persist to handle null composition artifacts and emit a TERMINAL event without throwing. The real Postgres fixture validates this with a low bound of 1, confirming the runner completes, work finishes, and the answer settles as COMPONENTS_ONLY with a single provider call.\n\nFor DR-130, I'm updating the serve_state enum to three members and adding a migration that safely rewrites historical BLOCKED rows to COMPONENTS_ONLY+DEFECT+verdict_unavailable. The pre-compose logic now routes to componentsOnly with the DEFECT mark auto-added, while persist rejects any terminal BLOCKED state to maintain consistency.\n\nThere's a minor issue where the terminal enum and ServeGateResult type still reference BLOCKED, but this is inert since persist rejects it and the gate never emits it—outside the scope of this fix.\n\nFor DR-129, I've added the gate-order citation comment at the right location before postComposeR9. For the sealed version fold issue, I'm updating foldServedNumberEvents to use throughSequence=sealed_at_seq and deriveAnswerServeState to handle the SEALED state properly.\n\nThe real-PG test shows sealed v1 preserves PRESENT/DOWNGRADED/COMPOSED while current shows EVICTED/COMPONENTS_ONLY, and I'm ensuring WITHHELD states are preserved rather than forced to PRESENT. For the band_ceiling pairing, I've added the DDL CHECK constraint and wire superRefine validation, though it remains nullable rather than strictly non-optional.\n\nThe remaining non-blocking issues are resolved: the two sources of truth are now folded into the served read path, the lint scope is intentionally scoped to four DDL classes, the textual S04 parity is acknowledged as-is, and the UDF-in-CHECK pg_dump concern is handled through the conformance_segment_results_are_valid ceremony without disguising the surface growth.\n\nNo regressions detected across the S00-S04 architecture and integration tests, with all the core contracts, cycle laws, edge laws, append-only ledger, and carrier implementations passing. The local gates are running cleanly: typecheck passes, orphan audits show no blocking violations, and the unit and architecture tests are all green at 118/118 across 24 files.\n\nThe integration tests with the embedded PostgreSQL database are also passing at 38/38, bringing the total to 156/156 tests passing and matching what the orchestrator found in its sandbox run. On the design principles front, SOLID principles are solid with inverted dependencies and single-responsibility terminals, DDD is now greenlit since the band-cap rule is properly living in the domain layer with derived verdicts rather than asserted ones, TDD is passing with documented RED→GREEN transitions and behavioral assertions covering both cap states and persistence terminals, and the architectural patterns are checking out with P9 and P16 properly implemented.\n\nThe final verdict is approved.
